function v174AiDirIndex(e){
  let dx=P.x-e.x,dy=P.y-e.y;
  if(Math.abs(e.aiMoveX)+Math.abs(e.aiMoveY)>.15){dx=e.aiMoveX;dy=e.aiMoveY}
  const a=Math.atan2(dy,dx),oct=Math.round(a/(Math.PI/4));
  // atan2: E0, SE1, S2, SW3, W4, NW-3, N-2, NE-1 -> canonical N..NW.
  return ((oct+2)+8)%8;
}
function v174AiSpriteCfg(e){
  let a=e.aiAnim||'idle',cfg=null,rowMap=null,scale=1.8,foot=.91;
  if(e.aiClass==='gnome'){cfg=GNOME_ANIM[a];rowMap=GNOME_SOURCE_ROW;scale=GNOME_DRAW_SCALE;foot=113/128}
  else if(e.aiClass==='archer'){cfg=ARCHER_ANIM[a];rowMap=ARCHER_SOURCE_ROW;scale=ARCHER_DRAW_SCALE;foot=184/224}
  else if(e.aiClass==='assassin'){cfg=ASSASSIN_ANIM[a];rowMap=(a==='run'?ASSASSIN_RUN_SOURCE_ROW:ASSASSIN_4DIR_ROW);scale=(a==='idle'?ASSASSIN_IDLE_DRAW_SCALE:(a==='attack'?ASSASSIN_ATTACK_DRAW_SCALE:ASSASSIN_RUN_DRAW_SCALE));foot=156/192}
  else if(e.aiClass==='tank'){cfg=TANK_ANIM[a];rowMap=TANK_4DIR_ROW;scale=TANK_DRAW_SCALE;foot=232/256}
  else if(e.aiClass==='barbarian'){cfg=BERSERKER_ANIM[a];rowMap=BERSERKER_4DIR_ROW;scale=BERSERKER_DRAW_SCALE;foot=232/256}
  else if(e.aiClass==='priest'){cfg=PRIEST_ANIM[a];rowMap=PRIEST_4DIR_ROW;scale=PRIEST_DRAW_SCALE;foot=236/256}
  else if(e.aiClass==='mage'){cfg=MAGE_ANIM[a];rowMap=MAGE_4DIR_ROW;scale=MAGE_DRAW_SCALE;foot=236/256}
  else if(e.aiClass==='paladin'){cfg=PALADIN_ANIM[a];rowMap=PALADIN_4DIR_ROW;scale=PALADIN_DRAW_SCALE;foot=234/256}
  return {anim:cfg,rowMap:rowMap,scale:scale,foot:foot};
}
function v174DrawAiTrainingFighter(e,sx,sy){
  const now=Date.now(),c=V174_AI_CLASS[e.aiClass]||V174_AI_CLASS.assassin;
  const cfg=v174AiSpriteCfg(e),a=cfg.anim,di=v174AiDirIndex(e);
  const bob=Math.sin(e.bob)*2;
  const _aiHidden=(e.aiClass==='assassin'&&now<(e.aiHiddenUntil||0));
  cx.save();
  if(_aiHidden)cx.globalAlpha=.12;
  const aiVisualSize=phoneCharacterBodySize(e.sz,1);
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.42)';cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,sy+aiVisualSize*.45,aiVisualSize*.38,aiVisualSize*.14,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  if(a&&a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0){
    const frame=Math.floor(now/(1000/Math.max(1,a.fps)))%a.frames;
    const row=cfg.rowMap?cfg.rowMap[di]:0;
    const dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(e.sz*cfg.scale,1),dw=dh;
    cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
    if(e.flash>0)cx.filter='brightness(1.65)';
    cx.drawImage(a.img,frame*a.fw,row*a.fh,a.fw,a.fh,sx-dw/2,sy+e.sz*.40-cfg.foot*dh+bob,dw,dh);
    cx.filter='none';
  }else{
    cx.fillStyle='#9d63db';cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,sy-10,aiVisualSize*.35,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  }
  cx.globalAlpha=1;
  if(_aiHidden&&now>(e.aiSmokeLabelUntil||0)){
    cx.globalAlpha=.22;cx.strokeStyle='#8d8d9a';cx.setLineDash([4,5]);
    cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,sy+e.sz*.35,e.sz*.48,e.sz*.18,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.stroke();
    cx.setLineDash([]);cx.restore();return;
  }
  const bw=Math.max(78,e.sz*1.25),by=sy-e.sz*.86;
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.72)';cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,by,bw,5);
  cx.fillStyle='#d04fff';cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,by,bw*Math.max(0,e.hp/e.mhp),5);
  cx.font='bold 10px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';cx.fillStyle='#e7c8ff';
  cx.fillText('ИИ · '+c.name+' · '+['','I','II','III'][e.aiRank],sx,by-6);

  // Compact skill indicator: small "skill button" + its real name.
  if(now<(e.aiSkillBadgeUntil||0)&&e.aiCastName){
    const idx=Number.isInteger(e.aiSkillIndex)?e.aiSkillIndex:-1;
    const tag=idx>=0?String(idx+1):'A';
    const txt=e.aiCastName==='Обычная атака'?'АТАКА':e.aiCastName;
    const tw=Math.min(150,Math.max(64,cx.measureText(txt).width+35));
    const bx=sx-tw/2,yy=by-31;
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(8,8,10,.86)';cx.fillRect(bx,yy,tw,18);
    cx.strokeStyle=idx>=0?'rgba(255,197,88,.95)':'rgba(215,215,215,.75)';
    cx.lineWidth=1;cx.strokeRect(bx+.5,yy+.5,tw-1,17);
    cx.fillStyle=idx>=0?'#8b4f18':'#454545';cx.fillRect(bx+3,yy+3,13,12);
    cx.fillStyle='#fff2c9';cx.font='bold 8px monospace';cx.fillText(tag,bx+9.5,yy+12);
    cx.fillStyle='#ffd783';cx.font='bold 8px monospace';
    cx.fillText(txt.slice(0,22),bx+19+(tw-22)/2,yy+12);
  }

  // Tiny, cheap hit stroke: ranged attacks visibly connect to the target;
  // melee attacks produce a short slash at the player's feet.
  if(now<(e.aiHitFxUntil||0)){
    const px=P.x-cam.x,py=P.y-cam.y;
    const alpha=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,((e.aiHitFxUntil||0)-now)/170));
    cx.save();cx.globalAlpha=alpha;
    cx.strokeStyle=e.aiHitFxType==='magic'?'#b98cff':'#ffbd62';
    cx.lineWidth=e.aiHitFxCrit?3:2;
    if(c.range>150){
      cx.beginPath();cx.moveTo(sx,sy-e.sz*.12);cx.lineTo(px,py-e.sz*.18);cx.stroke();
    }else{
      cx.beginPath();cx.moveTo(px-13,py-18);cx.lineTo(px+14,py+2);
      cx.moveTo(px+11,py-19);cx.lineTo(px-9,py+3);cx.stroke();
    }
    cx.restore();
  }

  cx.textAlign='left';cx.restore();
}

let __ppaHudNextAt=0;
function update(){
  PPAOnlineTick();
  ppaCleanupDeathRemnants();
  updateConsumableHud(false);
  updatePremiumRegen();
  if(P.dead)return;
  v174AiTickPlayerDebuffs();

  const _smartMove=updateSmartAttackInput();
  // V139: Smart Attack only supplies movement when joystick is neutral.
  // Any live joystick input keeps full manual movement while target lock stays active.
  const _moveX=_smartMove?_smartMove.x:jX;
  const _moveY=_smartMove?_smartMove.y:jY;

  // V174: class-AI crowd control is numeric only (no particle FX).
  const _aiMoveMul=Date.now()<(P.aiRootUntil||0)?0:(Date.now()<(P.aiSlowUntil||0)?Math.max(.30,Number(P.aiSlowMul)||.55):1);
  P.vx=_moveX*P.sp*shopSpeedMul()*_aiMoveMul;
  P.vy=_moveY*P.sp*shopSpeedMul()*_aiMoveMul;
  let nx=P.x+P.vx, ny=P.y+P.vy;

  // Keep in bounds (per scene)
  const sc=SCENES[P.scene];
  nx=Math.max(sc.bounds.minX,Math.min(sc.bounds.maxX,nx));
  ny=Math.max(sc.bounds.minY,Math.min(sc.bounds.maxY,ny));

  if(P.scene==='arena'){
    // Stay on the circular arena floor — no wandering into the black void
    const c=sc.center,rad=460;
    const dx=nx-c.x,dy=ny-c.y,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
    if(dist>rad){nx=c.x+dx/dist*rad;ny=c.y+dy/dist*rad}
  }else if(P.scene==='pvp1'||P.scene==='pvpteam'){
    const mv=pvpSlide(P.scene,P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
    // Du
===== DEF MAGE_ANIM #6 @ 98221516 =====
a=.25;cx.fillStyle=c;cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,sy,15,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();cx.globalAlpha=1;
  if(im&&im.complete&&im.naturalWidth>0){cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;cx.drawImage(im,sx-12,sy-12,24,24)}
  else{cx.fillStyle=c;cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,sy,6,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill()}cx.restore();
}


// === V204 NICKNAME ABOVE PLAYER ===========================================
function ppaPlayerNickname(){
  try{
    var n=(typeof INV!=='undefined'&&INV&&String(INV.playerName||'').trim())
      ||String(window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||'').trim();
    return n||'';
  }catch(_){return String(window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||'').trim()}
}
function ppaPlayerVisualTopScreenY(){
  var sy=P.y-cam.y;
  var _sv=P.scene==='clansiege'?CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE:1;
  var bob=Math.sin(P.bob)*(playerUsesGnomeSprites()?2.0:3)*_sv;
  var dh,dy;
  if(playerUsesGnomeSprites()){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*GNOME_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(113/128)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesArcherSprites()){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*ARCHER_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(184/224)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&ASSASSIN_ANIM[P.anim]){
    var sc=(P.anim==='idle')?ASSASSIN_IDLE_DRAW_SCALE:(P.anim==='attack'?ASSASSIN_ATTACK_DRAW_SCALE:ASSASSIN_RUN_DRAW_SCALE);
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*sc,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(156/192)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesTankSprites()&&TANK_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*TANK_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(232/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesBerserkerSprites()&&BERSERKER_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*BERSERKER_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(232/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesPriestSprites()&&PRIEST_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*PRIEST_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(236/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesMageSprites()&&MAGE_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*MAGE_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(236/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesPaladinSprites()&&PALADIN_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*PALADIN_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(234/256)*dh;
  }else{
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*1.4,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-dh;
  }
  return sy+(bob+dy)*_sv;
}
function ppaPlayerClanName(){
  try{
    var c=(typeof CLAN_LOCAL_STATE!=='undefined'&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan)
      ?String(CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan.name||'').trim():'';
    return c;
  }catch(_){return ''}
}
function drawPlayerNickname(){
  var name=ppaPlayerNickname();
  if(!name)return;
  var clan=ppaPlayerClanName();
  var sx=P.x-cam.x;
  var y=ppaPlayerVisualTopScreenY()-9;

  cx.save();
  cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.textBaseline='bottom';
  cx.lineJoin='round';

  if(clan){
    var clanText='['+clan.slice(0,18)+']';
    cx.font='700 8px Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif';
    cx.lineWidth=2.4;
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba(8,12,20,.94)';
    cx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.78)';
    cx.shadowBlur=3;
    cx.strokeText(clanText,sx,y-12);
    cx.shadowBlur=0;
    cx.fillStyle='#a9cfff';
    cx.fillText(clanText,sx,y-12);
  }

  cx.font='600 10px Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif';
  cx.lineWidth=2.6;
  cx.strokeStyle='rgba(18,8,5,.92)';
  cx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.72)';
  cx.shadowBlur=3;
  cx.strokeText(name.slice(0,18),sx,y);
  cx.shadowBlur=0;
  cx.fillStyle='#f2d39a';
  cx.fillText(name.slice(0,18),sx,y);
  cx.restore();
}

function drawPlayer(){
  const sx=P.x-cam.x,sy=P.y-cam.y;
  const siegeVisualScale=P.scene==='clansiege'?CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE:1;

  // V277: only the player's 8 classes are drawn 10 px smaller.
  // No collision, camera, NPC, mob, AI or combat values are changed.
  const PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX=10;
  const playerClassDrawHeight=(baseHeight)=>{
    const sc=Math.max(.01,Number(siegeVisualScale)||1);
    return Math.max(8,phoneCharacterDrawHeight(baseHeight,siegeVisualScale)-PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX/sc);
  };
  const playerClassBodySize=(baseSize)=>{
    const sc=Math.max(.01,Number(siegeVisualScale)||1);
    return Math.max(8,phoneCharacterBodySize(baseSize,siegeVisualScale)-PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX/sc);
  };
  const isGnome=playerUsesGnomeSprites();
  const isArcher=playerUsesArcherSprites();
  const isAssassinCustom=playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&!!ASSASSIN_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isTank=playerUsesTankSprites()&&!!TANK_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isBerserker=playerUsesBerserkerSprites()&&!!BERSERKER_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isPriest=playerUsesPriestSprites()&&!!PRIEST_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isMage=playerUsesMageSprites()&&!!MAGE_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isPaladin=playerUsesPaladinSprites()&&!!PALADIN_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isCustomDirectional=isGnome||isArcher||isAssassinCustom||isTank||isBerserker||isPriest||isMage||isPaladin;
  const phoneBodyBase=playerClassBodySize(P.sz);
  const visualBody=(isGnome?phoneBodyBase*0.72:phoneBodyBase)*siegeVisualScale;
  const bob=P.scene==='fartzone'?0:Math.sin(P.bob)*(isGnome?2.0:3);

  // Shadow follows the visible character size.
  // Fart Zone: exact feet-to-ground anchor, no visual hovering.
  const playerShadowY=P.scene==='fartzone'?sy+visualBody*0.40:sy+visualBody*0.45;
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.4)';
  cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,playerShadowY,visualBody*0.38,visualBody*0.14,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

  // Dust when running
  if((Math.abs(P.vx)>0.5||Math.abs(P.vy)>0.5)&&Math.random()<0.25)
    PT.push({x:P.x+(Math.random()-.5)*(isGnome?6:12)*siegeVisualScale,y:P.y+visualBody*0.35,
      vx:-P.vx*0.4+(Math.random()-.5),vy:-P.vy*0.2+(Math.random()-.5)*0.5,
      life:10,ml:10,sz:(isGnome?1.5:2)*siegeVisualScale,col:'rgba(120,100,70,0.4)'});

  cx.save();
  cx.translate(sx,sy+bob);
  if(P.scene==='clansiege')cx.scale(CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE,CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE);

  // Legacy lean/recoil is disabled for the dwarf because his approved
  // 4-frame attack already contains the physical movement.
  if(!isCustomDirectional){
    let lean=0;
    if(P.shootT>0)lean=P.face>0?0.12:-0.12;
    const rc=P.recoil*6*(P.face>0?-1:1);
    cx.translate(rc,0);
    cx.rotate(lean);
  }

  const cur=playerAnimDef(P.anim);
  const canonicalDir=isCustomDirectional?playerDir8():0;
  const dirRow=isGnome
    ? GNOME_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
    : (isArcher
        ? ARCHER_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
        : (isAssassinCustom
            ? (P.anim==='run'
                ? ASSASSIN_RUN_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
                : ASSASSIN_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir])
            : (isTank
                ? TANK_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                : (isBerserker
                    ? BERSERKER_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                    : (isPriest
                        ? PRIEST_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                        : (isMage
                            ? MAGE_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
        
===== DEF MAGE_ANIM #7 @ 98224268 =====
f';
    cx.lineWidth=2.4;
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba(8,12,20,.94)';
    cx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.78)';
    cx.shadowBlur=3;
    cx.strokeText(clanText,sx,y-12);
    cx.shadowBlur=0;
    cx.fillStyle='#a9cfff';
    cx.fillText(clanText,sx,y-12);
  }

  cx.font='600 10px Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif';
  cx.lineWidth=2.6;
  cx.strokeStyle='rgba(18,8,5,.92)';
  cx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.72)';
  cx.shadowBlur=3;
  cx.strokeText(name.slice(0,18),sx,y);
  cx.shadowBlur=0;
  cx.fillStyle='#f2d39a';
  cx.fillText(name.slice(0,18),sx,y);
  cx.restore();
}

function drawPlayer(){
  const sx=P.x-cam.x,sy=P.y-cam.y;
  const siegeVisualScale=P.scene==='clansiege'?CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE:1;

  // V277: only the player's 8 classes are drawn 10 px smaller.
  // No collision, camera, NPC, mob, AI or combat values are changed.
  const PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX=10;
  const playerClassDrawHeight=(baseHeight)=>{
    const sc=Math.max(.01,Number(siegeVisualScale)||1);
    return Math.max(8,phoneCharacterDrawHeight(baseHeight,siegeVisualScale)-PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX/sc);
  };
  const playerClassBodySize=(baseSize)=>{
    const sc=Math.max(.01,Number(siegeVisualScale)||1);
    return Math.max(8,phoneCharacterBodySize(baseSize,siegeVisualScale)-PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX/sc);
  };
  const isGnome=playerUsesGnomeSprites();
  const isArcher=playerUsesArcherSprites();
  const isAssassinCustom=playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&!!ASSASSIN_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isTank=playerUsesTankSprites()&&!!TANK_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isBerserker=playerUsesBerserkerSprites()&&!!BERSERKER_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isPriest=playerUsesPriestSprites()&&!!PRIEST_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isMage=playerUsesMageSprites()&&!!MAGE_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isPaladin=playerUsesPaladinSprites()&&!!PALADIN_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isCustomDirectional=isGnome||isArcher||isAssassinCustom||isTank||isBerserker||isPriest||isMage||isPaladin;
  const phoneBodyBase=playerClassBodySize(P.sz);
  const visualBody=(isGnome?phoneBodyBase*0.72:phoneBodyBase)*siegeVisualScale;
  const bob=P.scene==='fartzone'?0:Math.sin(P.bob)*(isGnome?2.0:3);

  // Shadow follows the visible character size.
  // Fart Zone: exact feet-to-ground anchor, no visual hovering.
  const playerShadowY=P.scene==='fartzone'?sy+visualBody*0.40:sy+visualBody*0.45;
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.4)';
  cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,playerShadowY,visualBody*0.38,visualBody*0.14,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

  // Dust when running
  if((Math.abs(P.vx)>0.5||Math.abs(P.vy)>0.5)&&Math.random()<0.25)
    PT.push({x:P.x+(Math.random()-.5)*(isGnome?6:12)*siegeVisualScale,y:P.y+visualBody*0.35,
      vx:-P.vx*0.4+(Math.random()-.5),vy:-P.vy*0.2+(Math.random()-.5)*0.5,
      life:10,ml:10,sz:(isGnome?1.5:2)*siegeVisualScale,col:'rgba(120,100,70,0.4)'});

  cx.save();
  cx.translate(sx,sy+bob);
  if(P.scene==='clansiege')cx.scale(CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE,CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE);

  // Legacy lean/recoil is disabled for the dwarf because his approved
  // 4-frame attack already contains the physical movement.
  if(!isCustomDirectional){
    let lean=0;
    if(P.shootT>0)lean=P.face>0?0.12:-0.12;
    const rc=P.recoil*6*(P.face>0?-1:1);
    cx.translate(rc,0);
    cx.rotate(lean);
  }

  const cur=playerAnimDef(P.anim);
  const canonicalDir=isCustomDirectional?playerDir8():0;
  const dirRow=isGnome
    ? GNOME_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
    : (isArcher
        ? ARCHER_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
        : (isAssassinCustom
            ? (P.anim==='run'
                ? ASSASSIN_RUN_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
                : ASSASSIN_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir])
            : (isTank
                ? TANK_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                : (isBerserker
                    ? BERSERKER_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                    : (isPriest
                        ? PRIEST_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                        : (isMage
                            ? MAGE_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                            : (isPaladin
                                ? PALADIN_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                                : (cur.rows ? getDirRow() : 0))))))));
  const useFlip=isGnome
    ? GNOME_FLIP_BY_DIR[canonicalDir]
    : (isArcher
        ? ARCHER_FLIP_BY_DIR[canonicalDir]
        : ((isAssassinCustom||isTank||isBerserker||isPriest||isMage||isPaladin) ? false : (dirRow===2 && P.face>0)));
  const frameIndex=((P.animFrame%cur.frames)+cur.frames)%cur.frames;

  if(cur.img.complete && cur.img.naturalWidth > 0){
    const fh_row=cur.fh;
    const fw_frame=cur.fw;
    const footY=P.sz*0.40;
    const srcY=dirRow*fh_row;

    let dh,dw,dx,dy;
    if(isGnome){
      // Approved dwarf size stays unchanged.
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*GNOME_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(113/128)*dh);
    }else if(isArcher){
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*ARCHER_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(184/224)*dh);
    }else if(isAssassinCustom){
      const assScale=(P.anim==='idle')
        ? ASSASSIN_IDLE_DRAW_SCALE
        : (P.anim==='attack' ? ASSASSIN_ATTACK_DRAW_SCALE : ASSASSIN_RUN_DRAW_SCALE);
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*assScale,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(156/192)*dh);
    }else if(isTank){
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*TANK_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(232/256)*dh);
    }else if(isBerserker){
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*BERSERKER_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(232/256)*dh);
    }else if(isPriest){
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*PRIEST_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(236/256)*dh);
    }else if(isMage){
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*MAGE_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(236/256)*dh);
    }else if(isPaladin){
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*PALADIN_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(234/256)*dh);
    }else{
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*1.4,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh*(fw_frame/fh_row);
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(footY-dh);
    }

    if(useFlip){
      cx.save();
      cx.scale(-1,1);
      cx.drawImage(cur.img,fw_frame*frameIndex,srcY,fw_frame,fh_row,dx,dy,dw,dh);
      cx.restore();

function playerUsesEightDirSprites(){

    (playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&P.anim==='run');
}
function playerAnimDef(name){
  if(playerUsesGnomeSprites() && GNOME_ANIM[name]){
    const a=GNOME_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return GNOME_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesArcherSprites() && ARCHER_ANIM[name]){
    const a=ARCHER_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return ARCHER_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesAssassinSprites() && ASSASSIN_ANIM[name]){
    const a=ASSASSIN_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return ASSASSIN_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesTankSprites() && TANK_ANIM[name]){
    const a=TANK_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return TANK_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesBerserkerSprites() && BERSERKER_ANIM[name]){
    const a=BERSERKER_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return BERSERKER_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesPriestSprites() && PRIEST_ANIM[name]){
    const a=PRIEST_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return PRIEST_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesMageSprites() && MAGE_ANIM[name]){
    const a=MAGE_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return MAGE_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesPaladinSprites() && PALADIN_ANIM[name]){
    const a=PALADIN_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return PALADIN_ANIM.idle;
  }
  return ANIM[name]||ANIM.idle;
}
// Canonical direction index: 0=N,1=NE,2=E,3=SE,4=S,5=SW,6=W,7=NW.
function dir8Canonical(dx,dy){
  if(Math.hypot(dx,dy)<0.001)return Number.isFinite(P.dir8)?P.dir8:4;
  let a=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  let n=(a+Math.PI/2)%(Math.PI*2);
  if(n<0)n+=Math.PI*2;
  return Math.round(n/(Math.PI/4))%8;
}
function playerDir8(){
  // Run-and-attack: movement continues in joystick direction, but the
  // character visually turns toward the shot/target for the attack moment.
  const aimingNow=(P.attacking||P.runAttackT>0||P.shootT>0);
  if(aimingNow && Number.isFinite(P.meleeAng)){
    P.dir8=dir8Canonical(Math.cos(P.meleeAng),Math.sin(P.meleeAng));
    return P.dir8;
  }
  if(Math.hypot(P.vx,P.vy)>0.15){
    P.dir8=dir8Canonical(P.vx,P.vy);
  }
  if(!Number.isFinite(P.dir8))P.dir8=4;
  return P.dir8;
}

  return typeof classKeyFromName==='function' && classKeyFromName(P.cls)==='archer';
}
function playerUsesAssassinSprites(){
  return typeof classKeyFromName==='function' && classKeyFromName(P.cls)==='assassin';
}
function playerUsesTankSprites(){

function playerUsesBerserkerSprites(){

    (playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&P.anim==='run');

function playerAnimDef(name){
  if(playerUsesGnomeSprites() && GNOME_ANIM[name]){
    const a=GNOME_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return GNOME_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesArcherSprites() && ARCHER_ANIM[name]){
    const a=ARCHER_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return ARCHER_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesAssassinSprites() && ASSASSIN_ANIM[name]){
    const a=ASSASSIN_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return ASSASSIN_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesTankSprites() && TANK_ANIM[name]){
    const a=TANK_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return TANK_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesBerserkerSprites() && BERSERKER_ANIM[name]){
    const a=BERSERKER_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return BERSERKER_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesPriestSprites() && PRIEST_ANIM[name]){
    const a=PRIEST_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return PRIEST_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesMageSprites() && MAGE_ANIM[name]){
    const a=MAGE_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return MAGE_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesPaladinSprites() && PALADIN_ANIM[name]){
    const a=PALADIN_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return PALADIN_ANIM.idle;
  }
  return ANIM[name]||ANIM.idle;
}
// Canonical direction index: 0=N,1=NE,2=E,3=SE,4=S,5=SW,6=W,7=NW.
function dir8Canonical(dx,dy){
  if(Math.hypot(dx,dy)<0.001)return Number.isFinite(P.dir8)?P.dir8:4;
  let a=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  let n=(a+Math.PI/2)%(Math.PI*2);
  if(n<0)n+=Math.PI*2;
  return Math.round(n/(Math.PI/4))%8;
}
function playerDir8(){
  // Run-and-attack: movement continues in joystick direction, but the
  // character visually turns toward the shot/target for the attack moment.
  const aimingNow=(P.attacking||P.runAttackT>0||P.shootT>0);
  if(aimingNow && Number.isFinite(P.meleeAng)){
    P.dir8=dir8Canonical(Math.cos(P.meleeAng),Math.sin(P.meleeAng));
    return P.dir8;
  }
  if(Math.hypot(P.vx,P.vy)>0.15){
    P.dir8=dir8Canonical(P.vx,P.vy);
  }
  if(!Number.isFinite(P.dir8))P.dir8=4;
  return P.dir8;
}

function dir8Canonical(dx,dy){

function playerDir8(){
  // Run-and-attack: movement continues in joystick direction, but the
  // character visually turns toward the shot/target for the attack moment.
  const aimingNow=(P.attacking||P.runAttackT>0||P.shootT>0);
  if(aimingNow && Number.isFinite(P.meleeAng)){
    P.dir8=dir8Canonical(Math.cos(P.meleeAng),Math.sin(P.meleeAng));
    return P.dir8;
  }
  if(Math.hypot(P.vx,P.vy)>0.15){
    P.dir8=dir8Canonical(P.vx,P.vy);
  }
  if(!Number.isFinite(P.dir8))P.dir8=4;
  return P.dir8;
}

function v174AiDirIndex(e){
  let dx=P.x-e.x,dy=P.y-e.y;
  if(Math.abs(e.aiMoveX)+Math.abs(e.aiMoveY)>.15){dx=e.aiMoveX;dy=e.aiMoveY}
  const a=Math.atan2(dy,dx),oct=Math.round(a/(Math.PI/4));
  // atan2: E0, SE1, S2, SW3, W4, NW-3, N-2, NE-1 -> canonical N..NW.
  return ((oct+2)+8)%8;
}
function v174AiSpriteCfg(e){
  let a=e.aiAnim||'idle',cfg=null,rowMap=null,scale=1.8,foot=.91;
  if(e.aiClass==='gnome'){cfg=GNOME_ANIM[a];rowMap=GNOME_SOURCE_ROW;scale=GNOME_DRAW_SCALE;foot=113/128}
  else if(e.aiClass==='archer'){cfg=ARCHER_ANIM[a];rowMap=ARCHER_SOURCE_ROW;scale=ARCHER_DRAW_SCALE;foot=184/224}
  else if(e.aiClass==='assassin'){cfg=ASSASSIN_ANIM[a];rowMap=(a==='run'?ASSASSIN_RUN_SOURCE_ROW:ASSASSIN_4DIR_ROW);scale=(a==='idle'?ASSASSIN_IDLE_DRAW_SCALE:(a==='attack'?ASSASSIN_ATTACK_DRAW_SCALE:ASSASSIN_RUN_DRAW_SCALE));foot=156/192}
  else if(e.aiClass==='tank'){cfg=TANK_ANIM[a];rowMap=TANK_4DIR_ROW;scale=TANK_DRAW_SCALE;foot=232/256}
  else if(e.aiClass==='barbarian'){cfg=BERSERKER_ANIM[a];rowMap=BERSERKER_4DIR_ROW;scale=BERSERKER_DRAW_SCALE;foot=232/256}
  else if(e.aiClass==='priest'){cfg=PRIEST_ANIM[a];rowMap=PRIEST_4DIR_ROW;scale=PRIEST_DRAW_SCALE;foot=236/256}
  else if(e.aiClass==='mage'){cfg=MAGE_ANIM[a];rowMap=MAGE_4DIR_ROW;scale=MAGE_DRAW_SCALE;foot=236/256}
  else if(e.aiClass==='paladin'){cfg=PALADIN_ANIM[a];rowMap=PALADIN_4DIR_ROW;scale=PALADIN_DRAW_SCALE;foot=234/256}
  return {anim:cfg,rowMap:rowMap,scale:scale,foot:foot};
}
function v174DrawAiTrainingFighter(e,sx,sy){
  const now=Date.now(),c=V174_AI_CLASS[e.aiClass]||V174_AI_CLASS.assassin;
  const cfg=v174AiSpriteCfg(e),a=cfg.anim,di=v174AiDirIndex(e);
  const bob=Math.sin(e.bob)*2;
  const _aiHidden=(e.aiClass==='assassin'&&now<(e.aiHiddenUntil||0));
  cx.save();
  if(_aiHidden)cx.globalAlpha=.12;
  const aiVisualSize=phoneCharacterBodySize(e.sz,1);
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.42)';cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,sy+aiVisualSize*.45,aiVisualSize*.38,aiVisualSize*.14,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  if(a&&a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0){
    const frame=Math.floor(now/(1000/Math.max(1,a.fps)))%a.frames;
    const row=cfg.rowMap?cfg.rowMap[di]:0;
    const dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(e.sz*cfg.scale,1),dw=dh;
    cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
    if(e.flash>0)cx.filter='brightness(1.65)';
    cx.drawImage(a.img,frame*a.fw,row*a.fh,a.fw,a.fh,sx-dw/2,sy+e.sz*.40-cfg.foot*dh+bob,dw,dh);
    cx.filter='none';
  }else{
    cx.fillStyle='#9d63db';cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,sy-10,aiVisualSize*.35,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  }
  cx.globalAlpha=1;
  if(_aiHidden&&now>(e.aiSmokeLabelUntil||0)){
    cx.globalAlpha=.22;cx.strokeStyle='#8d8d9a';cx.setLineDash([4,5]);
    cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,sy+e.sz*.35,e.sz*.48,e.sz*.18,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.stroke();
    cx.setLineDash([]);cx.restore();return;
  }
  const bw=Math.max(78,e.sz*1.25),by=sy-e.sz*.86;
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.72)';cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,by,bw,5);
  cx.fillStyle='#d04fff';cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,by,bw*Math.max(0,e.hp/e.mhp),5);
  cx.font='bold 10px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';cx.fillStyle='#e7c8ff';
  cx.fillText('ИИ · '+c.name+' · '+['','I','II','III'][e.aiRank],sx,by-6);

  // Compact skill indicator: small "skill button" + its real name.
  if(now<(e.aiSkillBadgeUntil||0)&&e.aiCastName){
    const idx=Number.isInteger(e.aiSkillIndex)?e.aiSkillIndex:-1;
    const tag=idx>=0?String(idx+1):'A';
    const txt=e.aiCastName==='Обычная атака'?'АТАКА':e.aiCastName;
    const tw=Math.min(150,Math.max(64,cx.measureText(txt).width+35));
    const bx=sx-tw/2,yy=by-31;
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(8,8,10,.86)';cx.fillRect(bx,yy,tw,18);
    cx.strokeStyle=idx>=0?'rgba(255,197,88,.95)':'rgba(215,215,215,.75)';
    cx.lineWidth=1;cx.strokeRect(bx+.5,yy+.5,tw-1,17);
    cx.fillStyle=idx>=0?'#8b4f18':'#454545';cx.fillRect(bx+3,yy+3,13,12);
    cx.fillStyle='#fff2c9';cx.font='bold 8px monospace';cx.fillText(tag,bx+9.5,yy+12);
    cx.fillStyle='#ffd783';cx.font='bold 8px monospace';
    cx.fillText(txt.slice(0,22),bx+19+(tw-22)/2,yy+12);
  }

  // Tiny, cheap hit stroke: ranged attacks visibly connect to the target;
  // melee attacks produce a short slash at the player's feet.
  if(now<(e.aiHitFxUntil||0)){
    const px=P.x-cam.x,py=P.y-cam.y;
    const alpha=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,((e.aiHitFxUntil||0)-now)/170));
    cx.save();cx.globalAlpha=alpha;
    cx.strokeStyle=e.aiHitFxType==='magic'?'#b98cff':'#ffbd62';
    cx.lineWidth=e.aiHitFxCrit?3:2;
    if(c.range>150){
      cx.beginPath();cx.moveTo(sx,sy-e.sz*.12);cx.lineTo(px,py-e.sz*.18);cx.stroke();
    }else{
      cx.beginPath();cx.moveTo(px-13,py-18);cx.lineTo(px+14,py+2);
      cx.moveTo(px+11,py-19);cx.lineTo(px-9,py+3);cx.stroke();
    }
    cx.restore();
  }

  cx.textAlign='left';cx.restore();
}

let loadedCount=0;
const totalRes=RESOURCES.length;
let gameStarted=false;
const loadBarFill=document.getElementById('loadBarFill');
const loadText=document.getElementById('loadText');
const loadDetail=document.getElementById('loadDetail');
const loadPct=document.getElementById('loadPct');
const loadingScreen=document.getElementById('loadingScreen');

function onResLoad(name){
  loadedCount++;
  const pct=Math.round((loadedCount/totalRes)*100);
  loadBarFill.style.width=pct+'%';
  if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent=pct+'%';
  loadText.textContent='\u0417\u0430\u0433\u0440\u0443\u0437\u043a\u0430... '+loadedCount+'/'+totalRes+' ('+pct+'%)';
  loadDetail.textContent='\u2713 '+name;
  if(loadedCount>=totalRes && !gameStarted){
    startGame();
  }
}

function onResError(name){
  console.warn('Failed to load: '+name+' — using fallback');
  loadedCount++;
  const pct=Math.round((loadedCount/totalRes)*100);
  loadBarFill.style.width=pct+'%';
  if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent=pct+'%';
  loadText.textContent='\u0417\u0430\u0433\u0440\u0443\u0437\u043a\u0430... '+loadedCount+'/'+totalRes+' ('+pct+'%)';
  loadDetail.textContent='\u26a0 '+name+' (fallback)';
  if(loadedCount>=totalRes && !gameStarted){
    startGame();
  }
}

// Start loading each resource
RESOURCES.forEach(function(r){
  r.img.onload=function(){onResLoad(r.name)};
  r.img.onerror=function(){onResError(r.name)};
  // Set src AFTER attaching handlers to ensure we catch the event
  r.img.src=r.src;
});

// Safety timeout: start game after 8 seconds even if some resources fail
setTimeout(function(){
  if(!gameStarted){
    console.warn('Resource load timeout — starting with fallbacks');
    startGame();
  }
},8000);

var __gameLoopStarted=false;
function startGame(){
  if(gameStarted)return;
  gameStarted=true;
  try{loadBarFill.style.width='100%';if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent='100%';loadText.textContent='Готово';loadDetail.textContent='Мир готов';}catch(_){}
  loadingScreen.style.transition='opacity 0.5s';
  loadingScreen.style.opacity='0';
  setTimeout(function(){
    loadingScreen.style.display='none';
    Promise.resolve(typeof ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap==='function'?ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap():false)
      .then(function(handled){
        if(handled)return;
        var fresh=ppaConsumeFreshStandaloneCharacter();
        if(fresh){
          INV.playerName=fresh.nickname;window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=fresh.nickname;
          try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',fresh.nickname)}catch(_){}
          applyClass({name:fresh.className});beginGame();
          return;
        }
        document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='block';
      })
      .catch(function(){
        document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='block';
      });
  },500);
}

let loadedCount=0;
const totalRes=RESOURCES.length;
let gameStarted=false;
const loadBarFill=document.getElementById('loadBarFill');
const loadText=document.getElementById('loadText');
const loadDetail=document.getElementById('loadDetail');
const loadPct=document.getElementById('loadPct');
const loadingScreen=document.getElementById('loadingScreen');

function onResLoad(name){
  loadedCount++;
  const pct=Math.round((loadedCount/totalRes)*100);
  loadBarFill.style.width=pct+'%';
  if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent=pct+'%';
  loadText.textContent='\u0417\u0430\u0433\u0440\u0443\u0437\u043a\u0430... '+loadedCount+'/'+totalRes+' ('+pct+'%)';
  loadDetail.textContent='\u2713 '+name;
  if(loadedCount>=totalRes && !gameStarted){
    startGame();
  }
}

function onResError(name){
  console.warn('Failed to load: '+name+' — using fallback');
  loadedCount++;
  const pct=Math.round((loadedCount/totalRes)*100);
  loadBarFill.style.width=pct+'%';
  if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent=pct+'%';
  loadText.textContent='\u0417\u0430\u0433\u0440\u0443\u0437\u043a\u0430... '+loadedCount+'/'+totalRes+' ('+pct+'%)';
  loadDetail.textContent='\u26a0 '+name+' (fallback)';
  if(loadedCount>=totalRes && !gameStarted){
    startGame();
  }
}

// Start loading each resource
RESOURCES.forEach(function(r){
  r.img.onload=function(){onResLoad(r.name)};
  r.img.onerror=function(){onResError(r.name)};
  // Set src AFTER attaching handlers to ensure we catch the event
  r.img.src=r.src;
});

// Safety timeout: start game after 8 seconds even if some resources fail
setTimeout(function(){
  if(!gameStarted){
    console.warn('Resource load timeout — starting with fallbacks');
    startGame();
  }
},8000);

var __gameLoopStarted=false;
function startGame(){
  if(gameStarted)return;
  gameStarted=true;
  try{loadBarFill.style.width='100%';if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent='100%';loadText.textContent='Готово';loadDetail.textContent='Мир готов';}catch(_){}
  loadingScreen.style.transition='opacity 0.5s';
  loadingScreen.style.opacity='0';
  setTimeout(function(){
    loadingScreen.style.display='none';
    Promise.resolve(typeof ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap==='function'?ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap():false)
      .then(function(handled){
        if(handled)return;
        var fresh=ppaConsumeFreshStandaloneCharacter();
        if(fresh){
          INV.playerName=fresh.nickname;window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=fresh.nickname;
          try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',fresh.nickname)}catch(_){}
          applyClass({name:fresh.className});beginGame();
          return;
        }
        document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='block';
      })
      .catch(function(){
        document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='block';
      });
  },500);
}

let loadedCount=0;
const totalRes=RESOURCES.length;
let gameStarted=false;
const loadBarFill=document.getElementById('loadBarFill');
const loadText=document.getElementById('loadText');
const loadDetail=document.getElementById('loadDetail');
const loadPct=document.getElementById('loadPct');
const loadingScreen=document.getElementById('loadingScreen');

function onResLoad(name){
  loadedCount++;
  const pct=Math.round((loadedCount/totalRes)*100);
  loadBarFill.style.width=pct+'%';
  if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent=pct+'%';
  loadText.textContent='\u0417\u0430\u0433\u0440\u0443\u0437\u043a\u0430... '+loadedCount+'/'+totalRes+' ('+pct+'%)';
  loadDetail.textContent='\u2713 '+name;
  if(loadedCount>=totalRes && !gameStarted){
    startGame();
  }
}

function onResError(name){
  console.warn('Failed to load: '+name+' — using fallback');
  loadedCount++;
  const pct=Math.round((loadedCount/totalRes)*100);
  loadBarFill.style.width=pct+'%';
  if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent=pct+'%';
  loadText.textContent='\u0417\u0430\u0433\u0440\u0443\u0437\u043a\u0430... '+loadedCount+'/'+totalRes+' ('+pct+'%)';
  loadDetail.textContent='\u26a0 '+name+' (fallback)';
  if(loadedCount>=totalRes && !gameStarted){
    startGame();
  }
}

// Start loading each resource
RESOURCES.forEach(function(r){
  r.img.onload=function(){onResLoad(r.name)};
  r.img.onerror=function(){onResError(r.name)};
  // Set src AFTER attaching handlers to ensure we catch the event
  r.img.src=r.src;
});

// Safety timeout: start game after 8 seconds even if some resources fail
setTimeout(function(){
  if(!gameStarted){
    console.warn('Resource load timeout — starting with fallbacks');
    startGame();
  }
},8000);

var __gameLoopStarted=false;
function startGame(){
  if(gameStarted)return;
  gameStarted=true;
  try{loadBarFill.style.width='100%';if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent='100%';loadText.textContent='Готово';loadDetail.textContent='Мир готов';}catch(_){}
  loadingScreen.style.transition='opacity 0.5s';
  loadingScreen.style.opacity='0';
  setTimeout(function(){
    loadingScreen.style.display='none';
    Promise.resolve(typeof ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap==='function'?ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap():false)
      .then(function(handled){
        if(handled)return;
        var fresh=ppaConsumeFreshStandaloneCharacter();
        if(fresh){
          INV.playerName=fresh.nickname;window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=fresh.nickname;
          try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',fresh.nickname)}catch(_){}
          applyClass({name:fresh.className});beginGame();
          return;
        }
        document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='block';
      })
      .catch(function(){
        document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='block';
      });
  },500);
}
